(39; original 1648): Summa, V. 6, Migne, 176. 132.

(40; original 1649): In Sent., IV. 4, 3. 3, Paris ed., XVI. 406, 410.

(41; original 1650): Seeberg p. 359, Summa, III. 66. 7, Migne, IV. 613 sq.; P. Lomb., IV. 3, 8, Migne, II. 845; Bonav., Brevil. VII., Peltier’s ed., p. 319, Duns Scotus. In IV. Sent., vol. XVI. 272. Gregory IX., on being asked by the archbishop of Drontheim whether a certain baptism administered with beer was valid, water not being at hand, replied in the negative. Potthast, 11,048. The synod of Aurillac, 1278, pronouced sweet, salt, or melted snow water proper material.

(42; original 1651): Quamvis tutius sit baptizare per modum immersionis, potest tamen fieri baptismus per modum aspersionis vel etiam per modum effusionis.

(43; original 1652): Confirmatio est quasi ultima consummatio baptismi. Th. Aq., III. 72. 11, Migne, IV. 693

(44; original 1653): Th. Aq., III. 73. 9, quotes Ezek. 3:8, "I have made thy forehead hard against their foreheads." He commends the custom whereby the candidate for confirmation is supported by another, for "though he be an adult in body he is not yet an adult spiritually."

(45; original 1654): Quasi omnis devotio in ecclesia est in ordine ad illud sacramentum. Duns Scotus as quoted by Seeberg, Dogmengesch., II. 113.

(46; original 1655): See Schaff’s Christ in Song, pp. 465 sqq. The verse, depicting the doctrine of transubstantiation, runs:—

Verbum caro, panem verum verbo carnem efficit

Fitque sanguis Christi merum; etsi sensus deficit

Ad firmandum cor sincerum sola fides sufficit.

(47; original 1656): Corpus et sanguis in sacramento altaris sub speciebus panis et vini veraciter continentur, transubstantiatis pane in corpore et vino in sanguinem potestate divina.

(48; original 1657): Migne, 171. 776.

(49; original 1658): See Schwane, p. 656.

(50; original 1659): Schwane, p. 641, and Rocholl under Rupert in Herzog, XV. 229 sqq

(51; original 1660): Th. Aq., III. 73. 4, Migne, IV. 701; Bonaventura, Brev. VI. 9, Peltier’s ed., 322, eucharistiae dedit in sacrificium oblationis, et sacramentum communionis et viaticum refectionis.

(52; original 1661): Hostia salutaris. Eph. 5:2, is quoted where the word hostia is used in the Vulgate for Christ’s sacrifice.

(53; original 1662): De euchar. vol. XIII. 668. Th. Aquinas, III. 74. 1, Migne, IV. 705, speaks of the Cataphrygae and Pepuziani as mixing with the dough of the sacramental bread the blood of children gotten by pricking their bodies, and also of the Aquarii who, from considerations of temperance, used only water.

(54; original 1663): He defined transubstantiation as transitus totalis substantiae in substantiam. Seeberg, p. 378.

(55; original 1664): Th. Aq., III. 75. 1, Migne, IV. 716, neque sensu, neque intellectu deprehendi potest sed sola fide. Bonaventura says, Brevil. VI 9, in specierum utraque continetur totaliter, non circumscriptibiliter, nec localiter sed sacramentaliter totus Christus.

(56; original 1665): Quamvis non sint substantia, habent virtutem substantiae. Th. Aq., III. 77. 6, Migne, IV. 755.

(57; original 1666): Th. Aq., III. 77. 7, Migne, IV. 756; Bonaventura, Brevil., 322.

(58; original 1667): Non solum caro sed totum corpus Christi, scilicet ossa, nervi et alia hujusmodi. Th. Aq., 76. 1, Migne, IV. 732. He lays stress upon the word "body," which is made up of constituent parts, and the "flesh" of John 6:56, he explains as standing for the body. Following the Aristotelian distinction of substance and form, Thomas Aquinas, Migne, IV. 726, and the other Schoolmen (see Schwane, p. 648) declared that the form of the bread and wine is also changed into the body and blood of Christ. The words forma and species are distinguished. The species of bread and wine remain, the forma disappears. Duns Scotus devoted much space to proving that a substance may have a variety of forms.

(59; original 1668): Sub utraque specie sacramenti totus est Christus. Th. Aq., 76. 2, Migne, 733. Sub utraque specie est unus Christus et totus et indivisus, scilicet corpus, et anima, et Deus. Bonaventura, Brevil. Vl. 9, Peltier’s ed., VII. 322.

(60; original 1669): In acceptatione sanguinis totum Christum, Deum et hominem, et in acceptatione corporis similiter totum accipimus. Ep. 4:107, Migne, vol. 159 p. 255. Anselm was making a distinction between the body and spirit of Christ, the spirit being represented by the blood and wine, and the body by the bread and flesh.

(61; original 1670): Summa, II. VIII., Migne, 176. 462, ego in ejusmodi divina secreta magis venerenda quam discutienda cerneo.

(62; original 1671): Summa, 81. 3, Migne, IV. 810-813. Anselm used the same words. Migne, 159. 255. Schwane agrees that this conception, that Christ ate His own body, was general among the Schoolmen, p. 645.

(63; original 1672): Rex sedet in coena turba, cinctus duodena

Se tenet in manibus et cibat ipse cibus.

(64; original 1673): So Hugo, II, 8. 4; the Lombard, XI. 8; Thomas Aquinas, 81. 2, Migne. pp. 811 sq. The delusion is called a fictio and also "Judas’communion." Synod of London, 1175. The argument is in clear contradiction to the meaning of the Gospel narratives on their face.

(65; original 1674): For this theological and metaphysical curiosity, see Th. Aq., 80. 3, Migne, 789, non tangit mus ipsum corpus Christi, secundum propriam speciem sed solum secundum species sacramentales … nec tamen animal brutum sacramentaliter corpus Christi manducat quia non est natum uti eo ut sacramento, unde non sacramentaliter sed per accidens corpus Christi manducat, etc. Alb. Magnus, In Sent., IV. 13. 38. Borgnet’s ed., XXIX. 397, Bonaventura, Sent., IV. 13. 2. 1, Peltier’s ed., V. 550.

(66; original 1675): Seeberg, p. 360.

(67; original 1676): The priest being the mediator. Summa, Migne, 176. 472.

(68; original 1677): Sacerdos gerit imaginem Christi, Th. Aq., III. 83. 1, Migne, IV. 830.

(69; original 1678): Th. Aq., Supplem. 37. 5, Migne, IV. 1062.

(70; original 1679): Ex seipso virtutem habet gratiam conferendi. Th. Aq., III. 79. 1, Migne, IV. 774.

(71; original 1680): Th. Aq., 79. 7, Supplem. III. 71. 10, Migne, IV. 782, 1246 sq.; Al. Magnus, I. 4, extended the benefits of the mass also to the glorified pro salute vivorum, pro requie defunctorum, pro gloria beatorum.

(72; original 1681): Summa, III. 83. 5 sq., Migne, IV. 844-853.

(73; original 1682): Th. Aq., III. 83. 5, Migne, IV. 850.

(74; original 1683): Non ex hoc desinit aliquis minister esse Christi quod est malus, Th. Aq., 82. 5, 7, Migne, IV. 821, 824; Anselm, ep. IV. 107, Migne, 159. 257, had said the same thing, nec a bono sacerdote majus, etc.

(75; original 1684): Th. Aq., III. 80. 12, Migne, IV. 809. Perfectio hujus sacramenti non est in usu fidelium sed in consecratione materiae.

(76; original 1685): Requiritur ut cum magna devotione et reverentia ad, hoc sacramentum accedat .... Eucharistia exigit actualem devotionem in suscipiente, Th. Aq., III. 80. 10, Supplem. III, 32. 4, Migne, IV. 805, 1038.

(77; original 1686): We cannot help feeling strongly with Harnack when he exclaims, "In its doctrine of the eucharist, the Church gave expression to all that she held dear,—her theology, her mystical relation to Christ, the communion of believers, the priesthood, sacrifice, not to that faith which seeks assurance and to which assurance is given,"Dogmengesch., II. 489 sq

(78; original 1687): Populus indiget medio ad Deum qui per seipsum accedere ad deum non potest., Th. Aq., III. 22. 4, Migne, IV. 219.

(79; original 1688): De euchar. VI. 3.

(80; original 1689): Councils of Würzburg, 1287, Paris, 1212, etc. See Hefele, V. 866

(81; original 1690): See artt. Fronleichnamsfest in Wetzer-Welte, IV. 2061 sqq., and Herzog, VI. 297 sqq. It was one of the first observances to call forth Luther’s protest. Köstlin, Leben Luthers, I. 560.

(82; original 1691): Ep., 535, Migne, 163. 442, qui panem absorbere non possunt, etc., quoted in Herzog under Kinderkommunion, X. 289.

(83; original 1692): Suppl., XXXII. 4, Migne, IV. 1038. The council of Trent anathematized those who hold the communion of children to be necessary.

(84; original 1693): Migne, 163. 142. See Smend, p. 7, for other witnesses. Smend’s book is a most thorough piece of work and is indispensable in the study of the subject. With the exception of some quotations, I depend upon him for the contents of these paragraphs.

(85; original 1694): Called intinctio. Hugo of St. Victor and Peter the Lombard were among the first to condemn the practice. Also the synod of London, 1175, Hefele, V. 688. See also V. 224 for the action of the synod of Clermont, 1095.

(86; original 1695): Albertus makes no mention of the matter in his De eucharistia and Com. on the Sentences. Peter Rokyzana, at the council of Basel in the fifteenth century, appealed to him in his argument for giving the cup to the laity.

(87; original 1696): Th. Aq., III. 80. 12, Migne, IV. 808 sq., nihil derogat perfectioni hujus sacr., si populus sumat corpus sine sanguine dummodo sacerdos consecrans sumat utrumque. So also Bonaventura, Sent., IV. 11. 2.

(88; original 1697): The synod of Lambeth, 1281, seems to have forbidden the cup to the laity; the synod of Exeter, 1287, to have positively enjoined it.

(89; original 1698): See Migne, 89. 525. For an interesting account of the different shapes of the chalice, see Enc. Brit., XIX. 185 sq. The earlier chalices had two handles and a small base, those of the thirteenth and fourteenth centuries no handles and a broad base. Some of the later chalices were very capacious.

(90; original 1699): See the interesting details given by Smend, pp. 18 sqq.

(91; original 1700): Vinum purum ... ut facilius sacrum corpus glutiat. Smend, who `gives elaborate details, pp. 43-75.

(92; original 1701): The object was to prevent the loss of any of the sacred element by expectoration or vomiting, per sputum vel vomitum. Chrysostom made a recommendation of this sort, Smend, 44.

(93; original 1702): Sent., IV. 11, 2, 2, Peltier’s ed., V. 496.

(94; original 1703): Caesar of Heisterb., Dial., IV. 16.

(95; original 1704): Dial., IX. 29, Strange’s ed., II. 186.

(96; original 1705): IX. 3.

(97; original 1706): IX. 35, Strange’s ed., II. 191.

(98; original 1707): Dial., IX. 15.

(99; original 1708): See Kaufmann, trans. of Caesar, II. 208-210.

(100; original 1709): De la Marche’s ed., pp. 266 sq.

(101; original 1710): Duns Scotus had spoken of the "satisfaction which is the doing of a laborious work," quae est executio operis laboriosi. Report IV. 16. 1, quoted by Schwane, p. 669.

(102; original 1711): Tertullian, de Poen, XII. So also Jerome. See the Lombard, Sent., XIV. 1, Migne, 868; Bonaventura, Sent., XIV. 1, Peltier’s ed., V. 553; Brevil., VI. 10, VII. 323; Th. Aq., III. 84. 6, Migne, IV. 862; Supplem., VI. 3. 936; Alb. Magnus, In Sent., Borgnet’s ed., XXIX. 404 sq.

(103; original 1712): The Lombard devotes two and a half times the space to penance that he does to the eucharist; Migne’s ed., pp. 868-899, as against pp. 856-868 on the eucharist; Hugo of St. Victor, Migne’s ed., 550-578, as against 462-471 on the eucharist; Th. Aquinas, Migne’s ed., 852-1023, as against 695-852 on the eucharist, and Bonaventura nearly four times as much space devoting to penance, Peltier’s ed., vol. V. 533-709, vol. VI. 1-129, and to the eucharist, vol. V. 415-533.

(104; original 1713): Absolvitur homo a peccato, et reunitur ecclesiae et reconciliatur Christo, mediante clavi sacerdotali, Bonaventura, Brevil., VI. 10, Peltier’s ed., VII. 323.

(105; original 1714): The Rheims Version translates the word "do penance," though not uniformly, thereby utterly confusing the English reader who involuntarily puts into the New Testament word the Church’s sacramental invention.

(106; original 1715): Poenitentia dicitur et sacramentum et virtus mentis, Lombard XIV. l, p. 869; Th. Aq., Migne, IV. 850 sqq. While we use two words, "repentance" and "penance," the Schoolmen use only the one word, poenitentia, thus mystifying the mind as if repentance of heart, or metanoiva, did not include the entire meaning of the original word.

(107; original 1716): Sent., XVII. 1, Migne, p. 880. The finished sacramental theory of penance owed not a little to the tract de vera et falsa poenitentia, composed perhaps in the twelfth century and foisted upon Augustine. Gratian inserted nearly all of it in his Decretals, as did Peter the Lombard. According to Lea, I. 210, the work was still quoted as Augustine’s as late as the seventeenth century. Lea regards it as the composition of two authors of the fifth and twelfth centuries.

(108; original 1717): This is shown by Müller’s notable work,Der Umschwung, etc. Abaelard’s statement presenting the old view, and the statement of Thomas Aquinas representing the new view, are given in Köhler, pp. 11-18.

(109; original 1718): Summa, III. 84. 7; Supplem., VIII. 1, Migne, IV. 864, 943.

(110; original 1719): Lombard, XVI. 1, Migne, p. 877; Alb. Magnus, Borgnet’s ed., XXIX. 536. Th. Aq., 90. 1, 2, Migne, IV. 912 sq., and Bonaventura, Brevil., VI.10, Peltier’s ed., VII. 323, also call the first three "the integral parts" of penance. So also Abaelard, Ethica, 17-24.

(111; original 1720): See Schwane’s strong condemnation of this opinion, which he declares to be beyond a doubt the Lombard’s, p. 662.

(112; original 1721): Timor servilis principium est attritionis, Alex. of Hales quoted by Schwane, p. 664. Th. Aquinas, Supplem., I. 2, Migne, IV. 919, is much more moderate than Alexander, Bonaventura, and Duns. Caesar of Heisterbach calls "servile fear a gift of God," Koeniger, p. 31. At the close of the Middle Ages, Gabriel Biel took the position that attrition is changed by confession and absolution into contrition. See Seeberg, Dogmengesch., II. 121.

(113; original 1722): See Hahn, p. 413; Schwane, p. 666. The council of Trent, XIV. 4 (Schaff’s Creeds, II. 145 sq.), adopted the word "attrition" and defined it as an imperfect contrition. The doctrine of attritio formed a centre of discussion in the warm debate over indulgences started by Janssen’s work and participated in by Kolde, Kawerau, Dieckhoff, etc. Harnmack is very severe upon the doctrine as the dry rot in the Catholic system, Dogmengesch., II. 482, 504 sqq.

(114; original 1723): Aquinas quotes Augustine’s definition, Supplem., VII.1, IX. 3, Migne, IV. 940, 954.

(115; original 1724): Migne, IV. 939.

(116; original 1725): Th. Aq., III. 87. 1, Migne, IV. 890; Supplem., VI. 1, 3, VIII. 3, Migne, IV. 934, 936, 945. With characteristic exhaustiveness, Thomas goes into the question whether a man can confess sins he has never committed, Migne IV. 936.

(117; original 1726): In Sent., IV. 17. 2, Peltier’s ed., V. 674, ante hanc determinationem hoc non erat heresis, etc. Albertus Magnus also declared it was not sufficient to confess to God only, Borgnet’s ed. XXIX. 603.

(118; original 1727): Th. Aq., Supplem., VII. 1, 2, Migne, IV. 943 sq.; Bonaventura, Sent., XVII. 3. 1, Peltier’s ed., V. 695. Caesar of Heisterbach speaks of confession to an unbeliever as efficacious in the article of death, provided the unbeliever does not ridicule the sacrament, Koeniger, p. 73.

(119; original 1728): Fourth Lat., can. 21, synods of Treves, 1227, Canterbury, 1236, etc.

(120; original 1729): See Hefele, VI, 30.

(121; original 1730): Supplem., XV. 3, Migne, IV. 978. Duns Scotus (quoted by Seeberg, 412) says, satisfaction is the voluntary return of an equivalent redditio voluntaria aequivalentis.

(122; original 1731): Schwane, p. 670.

(123; original 1732): Potestas solvendi et ligandi, i.e.ostendendi homines ligatos vel solutos, etc. IV. 18, 6, Migne, p. 887.

(124; original 1733): See the form used by Honorius of Autun about 1130, indulgentiam et absolutionem de omnibus ... tribuat vobis Pater et Filius et Sp. Sanctus et custodiet vos a peccatis et ab omnibus malis et post hanc vitam perducat vos in consortium omnium sanctorum. Lea, I. 206.

(125; original 1734): Summa, III. 84, 3, Migne, IV. 857. It was not sufficient to say, "The onmipotent God absolve thee," or "God bestow on thee absolution," etc.

(126; original 1735): De sacr., II. 14, 8, Migne, 176. 568 .... De potestate ligandi et solvendi.

(127; original 1736): So the synods of Treves 1227, Canterbury 1236, London 1237, etc. The unchastity of nuns came under the bishop’s jurisdiction.

(128; original 1737): Si non potest remittere quantum ad poenam temporalem, nullo modo remittere potest quod omnino contrarium dictus evangelii. Supplem., VIII. 2, Migne, IV. 988; Sent., IV. 20, 1, 1-5.

(129; original 1738): Beringer-Schneider, the chief Rom. Cath. writer on Indulgences, p. 2, defines an indulgence "as an act of mercy and goodness, a salvation by the order of the Church, an act of grace and forgiveness."

(130; original 1739): Kreuzablass, etc., pp. 10 sqq. Gottlob, p. xv, says indulgences occupy a central place in the political and religious life of the last three centuries of the Middle Ages.

(131; original 1740): Supplem., xxv-xxvii, Migne, IV. 1013 sqq. Lea devotes the entire third volume of his Hist. of Confession to a noteworthy discussion of indulgences.

(132; original 1741): See for the text Köhler, pp. 5 sq.

(133; original 1742): relaxatio, remissio, indulgentia de injuncta poenitentia, etc. See Brieger for these expressions, and Brieger and Lea for numerous examples of papal indulgences of this sort.

(134; original 1743): Ethica, XL. See Köhler, p. 8.

(135; original 1744): Potthast, 3799, 12938, 14122.

(136; original 1745): Luard’s ed., IV. 90, 643.

(137; original 1746): See Jusseraud, Engl. Wayfaring Life in the M. Ages, London, 1890, pp. 41 sqq., for many cases of indulgence for building bridges.

(138; original 1747): Sabatier, F. Bartholi de Assisio tractatus de indulgentia S. Mariae de Portiuncula, 1900.

(139; original 1748): See p. 366, Lea, III. 270 sqq., and Wetzer-Welte, Sabbatina.

(140; original 1749): Summa, IV. 83.1, relaxatio poenae debitae pro peccato, quoted by Brieger.

(141; original 1750): Th. Aq., Summa, III. 83, 1. quorum meritorum tanta est copia quod omnem poenam debitam nunc viventibus excedunt. See Gottlob, pp. 271 sqq.

(142; original 1751): Quanto plures ex ejus applicatione trahuntur ad justitiam, tanto magis accresit ipsorum cumulus meritorum. See Friedberg, Corp. Jur. can., II. 1304 sq.

(143; original 1752): Vere poenitentibus et confessis was the common formula.

(144; original 1753): Dr. Lea, III. 63, has shown the significance of this document.

(145; original 1754): Köhler, p. 27, quae securam et mundatam animam ab omni culpa et poena fecerunt.

(146; original 1755): See Sabatier, Fr. F. Bartholi, etc., in part reprinted by Köhler, pp. 27 sqq.

(147; original 1756): See a number of instances in Brieger and especially Lea, III. 55-80. Lea quotes Piers the Ploughman’s Crede to show that this expressed the popular belief.

The power of the Apostells they posen in speche

For to sellen the synnes for silver other mede

And pulchye a pena the purple assoileth

And a culpa also, that they may cachen

Money other money wothe and mede to fonge.

One of the most striking instances of this form of indulgence is the absolutio plenaria a poena et culpa issued by Alexander V. to the members of the council of Pisa, Von der Hardt, Conc. Const. III. 688.

(148; original 1757): In contrast to de fore dei, God’s tribunal. See Lea, II. 296-371, and Brieger.

(149; original 1758): Lea, III. 595 sq., and the instructions of Albert, abp. of Mainz, quoted by Brieger, nec opus est, quod contribuentes pro animabus in capsam sint corde contriti et ore confessi.

(150; original 1759): Schaff, Creeds, II. 205. Harnack, Hist. of Doctr., II. 511 sqq., expresses his moral indignation over the mediaeval theory of penance. Of attritio, sacramentum poenitentiae, and indulgentia, he exclaims, das ist die katholische Trias! "That is the Catholic triad!"

(151; original 1760): Legenda aurea, under All Souls and Francis d’Assisi. Temple Classics ed, VI. 113, V. 231.

(152; original 1761): Supplem., XXIX. 3, Migne, IV. 1027.

(153; original 1762): Th. Aq., Supplem., XXXII. 4, Migne. IV. 1038; Bonaventura, Brevil., VI. 11, Peltier’s ed., VII. 326.

(154; original 1763): Cologne, 1279; Lambeth, 1330, etc.

(155; original 1764): P. Lombardus, Sent., IV. XXIV. 9; Hugo of St. Victor, De sacr., II. 2, 5; Th. Aq., Supplem., XXXVII. 2, Migne, IV. 1056; Bonavent., Brevil., VI. 12.

(156; original 1765): Per modum complementi superponitur episcopatus, etc., Bonavent., Brevil., VI. 12. P. Lombardus, Sent., XXIV. 9, Migne, p. 904, speaks of a fourfold rank of bishops, viz. patriarchs, archbishops, metropolitans, and bishops. These, he says, are not orders but "the names of dignities and offices." The teaching of Duns Scotus is uncertain. In one place he asserts the episcopate must be a distinct order, the eighth, because the bishop alone can administer several of the sacraments. See Seeberg, p. 441. On the other hand, he quotes Jerome to show that the episcopate was instituted by the Church and is not a matter of divine law. See Schwane, p. 684. It is still unsettled by canon law whether the episcopate is a separate order or not. See Friedberg, Kirchenrecht, p. 150. The council of Trent did not formally decide the question, though it speaks of the hierarchy of bishops, priests, and deacons. See Schaff, Creeds, II. 186 sqq. Innocent III. placed the subdeacon among the major orders. Friedberg, p. 150. According to Philip Hergenröther, Kathol. Kirchenrecht, pp. 208 sq., the episcopate is at the present time universally regarded in the Rom. Cath. Church as a distinct clerical order.

(157; original 1766): Th. Aq., Supplem., XL. 1, Migne, 1071; Bonaventura, Brevil., VI. 12, Peltier’s ed., 327. The synods of London 1102, Soissons 1078, Rouen 1190, Fourth Lateran 1215, etc., decreed the tonsure must not be concealed.

(158; original 1767): Th. Aq., Supplem., XXXIV. 4, 5, Migne, 1045 sq., efficacia principaliter residet penes eum qui sacramentum dispensat.

(159; original 1768): Schwane, p. 681, says there was no development in the ritual of ordination during the Middle Ages. Thomas Aquinas refers to the imposition of hands only incidentally in his chapters on penance. Summa, III. 84, 3, Migne, IV. 850. The council of Florence, 1438, enjoined that the chalice and paten should be given at the consecration to some of the orders.

(160; original 1769): Th. Aq., Supplem., XXXVI. I, Migne, IV. 1051, si malus ordinatur nihilominus ordinationem habet.

(161; original 1770): For example the 9th (Hefele, V. 380) and 11th oecumenical Councils pronounced such judgment, naming the anti-popes. So also the synod of Piacenza, 1095, which declared invalid the ordinations of Wibert and other bishops.

(162; original 1771): Th. Aq., Supplem., XXXIX. 2, Migne, 1065. Episcopus in haeresin lapsus ... non amisit potestatem quam habebat ordines conferendi. Thomas is most emphatic on this question and goes on: Omnis protestas quae datur cum aliqua consecratione, nulla casu contingente tolli protest, etc .... Unde cum episcopalisprotestas cum quadam consecratione detur, oportet quod perpetuo maneat quantumcumque aliquis peccet, vel ab ecclesia praecidatur.

(163; original 1772): Th. Aq., Summa, III. 65, 2, Migne, IV. 598, quia minimum habet de spiritualitate.

(164; original 1773): Abaelard, Theol Christ., 31, conjugium non confert aliquod donum sicut cetera sacramenta faciunt sed tamen mali remedium est ... datur propter incontinentiam refraenendam. Hugo of St. Vict., De sacr., II. 11. 3, Migne, p.481, conjugium ante peccatum ad officium, post peccatum ad remedium. Alanus ab Insulis, Reg. Theol., 114, Migne’s ed., p. 681, conjugium sacramentum remedii contra incontinentiam. So also, Bonaventura, Brevil., Vl. 13; Th. Aq., Supplem., XLII. 2, Migne, IV. 1084; Summa, LXI. 2, Migne, p. 558.

(165; original 1774): Th. Aq., Supplem., XLII. 1, Migne, IV. 1083, benedictio sacerdotis quae est quoddam sacramentale.

(166; original 1775): These were supposed to be the "years of discretion." Supplem., LVIII. 5, Migne, IV. 1165. The synod of Nismes, 1096, forbade the marriage of girls under twelve. For cases of the marriage of princesses under twelve, see Eicken, pp. 448 sq.

(167; original 1776): "Just as the offspring of animals follow the nature of the mother. " Thomas instances the mule, Supplem., LII. 4, Migne, 1127.

(168; original 1777): This refers to a marriage in which one party is a Catholic and the other a heretic, Jew, or infidel.

(169; original 1778): Synods of London 1102, 1125, 1200, Fourth Lateran 1215, Treves 1227, Magdeburg 1261, etc. The synod of London, 1200, forbade either man or wife taking a long journey without the other’s consent. Thomas Aquinas took the position that marriages between a believer and an unbeliever are not to be allowed because they prevent the education and training of children in the worship of God, which is one of the chief objects of the sacrament. Supplem., LIX. 1, Migne, IV. 1167.

(170; original 1779): Th. Aq., Supplem., LXI. 2, Migne, IV. 1177. Thomas asserts that, before the carnalis copula takes place, the bond is a spiritual one and it may be broken by either party becoming spiritually dead, dying to the world and living unto God in a convent. After copulation the bond between man and wife is a carnal tie—vinculum carnale —and can be broken only by the death of the body.

(171; original 1780): Th. Aq., Supplem., LXII. 5, Migne, IV. 1184, non licet uni, altero vivente, ad aliam copulam transire. Either party may, however, enter a convent without seeking the consent of the other.

(172; original 1781): Carnalibus appetitis infecta, de conceptu. II. Migne, 158. 434

(173; original 1782): vitium concupiscentiae, quod est originale peccatum.

(174; original 1783): Post peccatum non valet fieri carnalis copula absque libidinosa concupiscentia quae semper vitium est et etiam culpa. P. Lomb., Sent., II. 31, 3.

(175; original 1784): Etsi anima non traducatur, quia virtus seminis non potest causare animam rationalem. Th. Aquinas, Summa, II. 81, 1, Migne, II. 629.

(176; original 1785): Carentia ... defectus originalis justitiae estoriginal peccatum. Schwane, p. 401; Th. Aq., Summa, II. 81, 5.

(177; original 1786): Spoliatio in gratuitis et vulneratio in naturalibus.

(178; original 1787): Summa, II. 82, 3, materialiter quidem est concupiscentia, formaliter vero est defectus orig. just. Vitium and corruptio are the words most frequently used for the moral character of sin. Hugo of St. Victor, De sacr., I. 28, Migne, 176. 299.

(179; original 1788): Peccatum orig. non contrahitur a matre sed a patre, etc. Summa, II. 81, 5.

(180; original 1789): P. Lomb., II. 42, 9; Alb. Magnus, Borgnet’s ed., XXVII. 663 sqq., etc.

(181; original 1790): P. Lomb., II. 42, enumerates them as inanis gloria, ira, invidia, acedia vel tristitia, avaritia, gastrimargia, luxuria. Albertus Magnus skilfully discusses whether there could be any more than seven. In Sent., II. 42, Borgnet’s ed., XXVII. 662 sqq.

(182; original 1791): Utrum aequalis fieret multiplicatio virorum et mulierum. In Sent., II. 20, 2, Peltier’s ed., III. 85. The three reasons which Bonaventura adduces to account for the differences in sex will have to be read in the original. He enters into the subject with the precision of statement and detail which is a characteristic of scholastic discussion. It is fair to say that he pronounced the question a difficult one and one upon which the physicians and natural philosophers of his day were much divided.

(183; original 1792): Sed dolo illo serpentino quo mulier seducta est, nullo modo arbitror illum potuisse seduci.

(184; original 1793): De sacr., I. 7, Migne, 176. 290.

(185; original 1794): In Sent. II. 22, E. Borgnet’s ed., XXVII. 377.

(186; original 1795): In Sent., II. 22, I. 3, Peltier’s ed., III. 123.

(187; original 1796): Man hatte Augustinische Formeln und gregorianische Gedanken. Loofs, p. 291. Schwane, p. 455, praises Thomas’ clear treatment of the doctrines of grace, and says he taught them as they are taught in the Catholic systems of dogmatics to-day. Loofs, Harnack, and Seeberg seem to me to go too far in ascribing to Thomas a de-Augustinianizing tendency. His plain statements of the necessity of divine grace and human inability are Augustinian enough. Passing from the study of Thomas’ theory of the sacraments, it is easy to put upon the statements about grace a Pelagian interpretation. The fairer way is to interpret his theory of the sacraments in the light of his teachings on the doctrine of grace.

(188; original 1797): meritum apud deum esse non potest, nisisecundum praesuppositionem divinae ordinationis. Summa, II. 114, I. Migne, II. 960.

(189; original 1798): Verum non potest cognosecre sine auxilio divino. Summa, II. 109, 2, 6, 7, Migne, II. 907 sqq.

(190; original 1799): Sanguis qui est pretium nostrae redemptionis non dicitur obtulisse diabolo sed deo. Summa, III. 48, 4, Migne, III. 44.

(191; original 1800): Mors Christi nos justificat, dum per eam charitas excitatur in cordibus nostris. Sent., III. 19, 1.

(192; original 1801): Per passionem Christi homo cognoscit quantum deus hominem diligat et per hoc provocatur ad eumdiligendum. Summa, III. 46, 3, Migne, III. 417.

(193; original 1802): In Sent., III. 20, Peltier’s ed., IV. 439. He attempts to show that he is not out of accord with Anselm, but he makes poor work of it. Anselm’s statement is absolute. Cur deus homo, II. 10.

(194; original 1803): Peltier’s ed., IV. 474 sqq.

(195; original 1804): De divisione gratiae. Summa, Migne, II. 927-960.

(196; original 1805): Tota justificatio impii consistit in infusione gratiae ... justif. fit, deo movente hominem ad justitiam. Summa, II. 113, 3, 7, Migne, II. 946. 952.

(197; original 1806): Hugo of St. Victor, Desacr. I. 10, 9, Migne, 176. 341 sqq.; P. Lombardus, Sent., III. 23, 24, Migne, pp. 295 sqq.; Bonavent., In Sent., III. 23, 24, Peltier’s ed., IV. 475 sqq.; Th. Aquinas, IV. 1-5, Migne, IV. 12 sqq; Alb. Magnus, In Sent., III. 23, 24, Borgnet’s ed., XXVIII. 408 sqq.

(198; original 1807): Aliud credere deo, aliud credere deum, aliud credere in deum. P. Lomb., III. 23, 4.

(199; original 1808): Spec. eccles., Migne, 172. 823.

(200; original 1809): Summa, IV. 4, 2, Migne, IV. 14, quoting 1 Cor. 13:12.

(201; original 1810): Charitas dicitur forma fidei, etc., IV. 4, 3. Such faith which is without love fides informis.

(202; original 1811): P. Lomb., III. 25, 3, Migne, p. 300.

(203; original 1812): Fides non remanet in homine postquam discredit unum articulum fidei. Summa, IV. 5. 3, I. 7 sqq., Migne, III. 63 sq.

(204; original 1813): In heretico discredente unum articulum fidei, non manet fides neque formata neque informis. IV. 5, 3, Migne, p. 63.

(205; original 1814): Summa, II. 113, 4, Migne, II. 948.

(206; original 1815): This is one of the charges brought with great vehemence against Luther and the Reformation by Denifle, Luther und Lutherthum, I. 374-456. He misunderstood or willfully misrepresented Luther, who never intended to detach a life of good works from faith as its necessary consequence.

(207; original 1816): Thomas Aquinas calls it locus dolorum and infernum damnatorum.

(208; original 1817): Profundus carcer respectu amoenitatis coeli et est aer iste caliginosus in quem detrusi sunt demones, etc. Alb. Magnus, In Sent., III. 22, C, 4, Borgnet’s ed., XXVIII. 393.

(209; original 1818): Ignis est in fortissima calididate ... cui nil est comparabile. Alb. Magnus, Borgnet’s ed., XXX. 597.

(210; original 1819): Gehenna illa quae stagnum ignis et sulphuris dicta est, corporeus ignis erit et cruciabit damnatorum corpora vel hominum vel daemonum. P. Lomb., Sent., IV. 44, 6. Absque dubietate corporeus ignis cruciat, etc. Alb. Mag., In Sent., Borgnet’s ed., XXX. 393. Ignis corporalis qui concremabit et affliget spiritus et etiam corpora ... sed semper affliget, alios plus alios minus, secundum exigentiam meritorum. Bonavent., Brev., VII. 6.

(211; original 1820): Probabile est, quod idem locus vel quasi continuus, sit infernus et limbus. Th. Aq., Migne, IV. 1222. Thomas calls the infernal regions interchangeably infernus and inferni. Alb. Magnus uses the neuter plural inferna. In Sent., III, 26, C. 392.

(212; original 1821): Th. Aq., Summa, III. 69, 6, originali peccato debebatur poena aeterna in limbo puerorum. Limbus means edge or border. Alb. Magnus also calls it limbus parvulorum, the region of the little ones. Borgnet’s ed., XXVIII. 392.

(213; original 1822): Pueris non adest spes beatae vitae, etc. Th. Aq., Supplem., p. 1223. divinae justitiae aequitas perpetualiter eos consolidat, ut nec proficiant, nec deficiant, nec laetentur, nec tristentur; sed semper per sic uniformiter maneant, etc. Bonavent., In Sent., II. 33, 2, 3, Peltier’s ed., III. 419.

(214; original 1823): In Sent., III. 22, I. 4 sqq., Peltier’s ed., IV. 467.

(215; original 1824): Suppl., Migne, III. 1222. The deepest hell—profundissimus infernus —is the place of the lost. Bonavent., Brevil., VII. 6, Peltier’s ed., VII. 339.

(216; original 1825): Summa, III. 52, 1, Migne, IV. 476.

(217; original 1826): Attolite portas, principes, vestras.

(218; original 1827): Post hanc vitam non est tempus gratiam acquirendi. Th. Aq., Summa, III. 52, 7; Suppl., Migne, IV. 1244.

(219; original 1828): Poena purgatorii est in supplementum satisfactionis quae non fuerat plene in corpore consummata. Th. Aq., Suppl., 71, 6 Migne, IV. 1242.

(220; original 1829): Th. Aq., Migne, IV. 1239.

(221; original 1830): Claritas, subtilitas, agilitas, et impossibilitas quae ... secundum majoritatem et minoritatem prius habitae charitatis. Brevil., VII. 7, Peltier’s ed, VII. 340

(222; original 1831): Deum per essentiam videre in quo consistit perfecta hominis beatitudo. Summa, III. 52, 5, Migne, IV. 482.

(223; original 1832): Serm., XI.

(224; original 1833): Proslog., XXIV. sqq.

(225; original 1834): An. 1206, Luard’s ed. of M. Paris, II. 497-512.

(226; original 1835): Dial, I. 32, Strange’s ed., I. 36-39.

(227; original 1836): See Walter, Die ersten Wanderprediger, etc., p. 49.

(228; original 1837): See Kaufmann, Thos. von Chantimpré, pp. 117 sq.

Best guess 对照表

正文及中文脚注中带上标 * 的外文为 best guess，尚未逐项核实；原始字串保留如下。

原字串 Best guess 语言 出现位置 说明
metanoevw μετανοέω Greek 正文第 80 段 旧字体中 v 表示前一元音的尖音符，metanoevw 对应 μετανοέω，意为改变心意、悔改。
metanoiva μετάνοια Greek 脚注 106 旧字体中 v 表示前一元音的尖音符，metanoiva 对应 μετάνοια，与上下文内心悔改之义相符。

  1. 从经院学者用来论述圣礼的篇幅，可以略知他们对这一题目的重视。圣维克托的雨果 (Hugo of St. Victor) 用了440栏，见米涅 (Migne) 版，176. 183–617；伦巴德 (the Lombard) 的《箴言集》(Sentences) 共462栏，其中90栏论圣礼；波拿文图拉 (Bonaventura) 的《神学体系》(System of Theology)，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 版，共3875页，其中1003页论圣礼；托马斯·阿奎那 (Thomas Aquinas) 的《神学大全》(Summa) 共4854栏，其中670栏论圣礼，见米涅版，IV. 543–1217。查尔斯·霍奇博士 (Dr. Charles Hodge) 的《系统神学》(System. Theol.) 共2260页，其中207页论圣礼；谢德博士 (Dr. Shedd) 的《教义神学》(Dogm. Theol.) 共1348页，其中25页；E. V. 格哈特博士 (Dr. E. V. Gerhart) 的《纲要》(Institutes) 共1666页，其中84页；A. H. 斯特朗博士 (Dr. A. H. Strong) 的《系统神学》(Sys. Theol.) 共600页，其中30页。↩︎

  2. 与彼得·伦巴德 (Peter the Lombard) 大致同时，也有其他人把圣礼数目定为七件，如罗兰杜斯 (Rolandus，后来的亚历山大三世) 在其《箴言集》(Sentences) 中，以及班贝格的奥托 (Otto of Bamberg) 在一篇1158年的讲道中；后者由其传记作者赫尔博德 (Herbord) 记载。↩︎

  3. 米涅 (Migne)，176. 127及后。雨果在论述五件圣礼之后，接着论述婚姻，但我看不出他曾称婚姻为圣礼。↩︎

  4. 《论圣礼》(De sacr.)，II. 9，米涅 (Migne)，176. 473。雨果将 aqua aspersionis，即掺盐的洒水，溯源于彼得之后第五位教皇亚历山大 (Alexander)。将灰撒在头上，即 susceptio cineris，他列入“较小的圣礼”，但在定义中称之为“教会礼仪”；对棕枝主日使用棕枝，他也如此称呼；米涅，176. 423。↩︎

  5. 米涅 (Migne)，IV. 597、1025。↩︎

  6. 赫尔根勒特 (Hergenröther)，《天主教教会法》(Kathol. Kirchenrecht.)，第667页及下页。↩︎

  7. 大阿尔伯特 (Alb. Magnus) 对此有长篇论述，题为“圣礼何以有七件”(Cur sint sacr. septem)，见《箴言集第四卷注》(In IV. Sent.)，I. 2，第XXIX卷，第6–11页。↩︎

  8. 参见波拿文图拉 (Bonaventura)，《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，VI. 3，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 版，VII. 314；托马斯·阿奎那 (Thomas Aq.)，《神学大全》(Summa)，米涅 (Migne) 版，IV. 594及下页。↩︎

  9. 波拿文图拉 (Bonaventura)，《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，VI. 3；托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，《神学大全》(Summa)，III. 63. 1，米涅 (Migne) 版，IV. 571。↩︎

  10. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，《神学大全》(Summa)，III. 62. 6，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 569：Sacramenta veteris legis non habebant in se aliquam virtutem qua operarentur ad conferendam gratiam justificantem，即旧律法的圣礼本身并无任何能力，可以藉此施予使人称义的恩典。引自托马斯《箴言集注》(Sentences) 的文字，见洛夫斯 (Loofs)，第301页。↩︎

  11. 《箴言集第四卷注》(In IV. Sent.)，I. 21，第XXIX卷，第37页。↩︎

  12. 阿伯拉尔 (Abaelard) 在《神学导论》(Introd. ad Theol.)，米涅 (Migne) 版，第984页，已引用这一定义。大阿尔伯特 (Albertus Magnus) 及雨果之后的其他经院学者，引用奥古斯丁之后，通常也引用雨果，例如彼得·伦巴德 (Peter the Lombard) 和托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 66. 1。↩︎

  13. 《论圣礼》(De sacr.)，I. 9. 4，米涅 (Migne)，176. 325。↩︎

  14. 《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，VI.，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 版，VII. 311–330。伦巴德 (the Lombard)、大阿尔伯特 (Alb. Magnus)、托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aquinas) 等人，也使用医药这一比喻。↩︎

  15. Interiorem sacramentorum effectum operatur Christus，即圣礼内在的功效由基督成就，III. 64. 3，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 583。↩︎

  16. Sacr. justificant et gratiam conferunt ex opere operato，即圣礼因礼仪本身的施行而使人称义，并赋予恩典。参见施瓦内 (Schwane)，第581页所列出处。↩︎

  17. 《神学大全》(Summa)，III. 62. 1，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 562：causa vero instrumentalis non agit per virtutem suae formae sed solum per motum quo movetur a principali agente，即工具因并不凭自身形式的能力行动，而仅凭主要施动者推动它的运动而行动。↩︎

  18. 米涅 (Migne)，IV. 568及下页。Virtus passionis Christi copulatur nobis per fidem et sacramenta，即基督受难的能力藉信心与圣礼而与我们联合。↩︎

  19. Ecclesia sicut sacramenta a Christo accepit sic ad fidelium salutem dispensat，即教会怎样从基督领受圣礼，也照样为信徒的救恩施行圣礼。《神学纲要》(Breviloq.)，VI. 5，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 版，VII. 316。↩︎

  20. 参见泽贝格 (Seeberg)，《邓斯·司各脱》(Duns Scotus)，第356–358页。↩︎

  21. Non requiritur bonus motus qui mereatur gratiam sed sufficit quod suscipiens non ponat obicem，即不需要有配得恩典的善的意向，只要领受者不设置障碍就已足够。《箴言集注》(In Sent.)，IV. 1. 6，转引自施瓦内 (Schwane)，第581页。Nisi impediat indispositio，即除非因缺乏适当准备而受到阻碍，转引自泽贝格 (Seeberg)，第343页。↩︎

  22. Susceptio est dispositio sufficiens ad gratiam，即领受本身就是获得恩典的充分准备。泽贝格 (Seeberg)，第349页。关于托马斯派 (Thomists) 与司各脱派 (Scotists) 在圣礼问题上的分歧，另见哈纳克 (Harnack)，II. 483。↩︎

  23. Ministri ecclesiae possunt sacramenta conferre etiamsi sint mali. 即教会的圣职人员即使是恶人，也能施行圣礼。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，米涅 (Migne) 版，IV. 586、821、824。↩︎

  24. Ministri non gratiam conferunt sua virtute, sed hoc facit Christus sua potestate per eos sicut per quaedam instrumenta，即圣职人员不是凭自己的能力赋予恩典，而是基督凭自己的权能，藉他们如同藉某些工具来成就此事。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 64. 5，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 586。↩︎

  25. 泽贝格 (Seeberg)，第350页。↩︎

  26. 《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 版，第317页。这个比喻得到详尽的发挥，作为中世纪虔敬讲道法的实例，十分有趣。↩︎

  27. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 65. 4，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 601。↩︎

  28. 另参邓斯·司各脱 (Duns Scotus) 的论述，见泽贝格 (Seeberg)，第338页。↩︎

  29. 《论圣礼》(De sacr.)，II. 9, 5，米涅 (Migne)，176. 325。potuit Deus hominem salvare si ista non instituisset, sed homo nullatenus salvari posset si ista contemneret. 即上帝即使没有设立这些，也能拯救人；但人若藐视这些，就绝不能得救。↩︎

  30. Janua omnium aliorum sacramentorum，即一切其他圣礼的门户。波拿文图拉 (Bonavent.)，《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，VII.，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 版，第318页；托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，《神学大全》(Summa)，III. 62. 6，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 569；《补编》(Supplem.)，XXXV. 1，米涅，IV. 1047。↩︎

  31. Baptismus qui est regeneratio hominis in vitam spiritualem，即洗礼是使人重生而进入属灵生命。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 66. 9；67. 3；68. 9；72. 1，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 617、626、646、678。↩︎

  32. Omne peccatum per baptismum tollitur，即一切罪都藉洗礼除去。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，69. 1，米涅 (Migne)，第652页。Baptismus institutus est contra vulnus originalis peccati，即洗礼是为医治原罪的创伤而设立的。里尔的阿兰 (Alanus ab Insulis)，《驳异端》(cont. haer.)，I. 39、43，米涅，210. 345、347。↩︎

  33. 《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，VI.，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 版，第318页；托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 66. 6，米涅 (Migne)，第611页：quicquid desit ad plenam invocationem trinitatis tollit integritatem baptismi，即对三位一体的呼求凡有任何不全，就会破坏洗礼的完整性。↩︎

  34. 他们获准在仪式中使用本地语言。见1227年特里尔 (Treves)、1233年美因茨 (Mainz) 的教会会议。祭司也受命用俗语教导平信徒洗礼仪式，使他们能在紧急情况下使用。见1243年弗里茨拉尔 (Fritzlar) 会议，赫费勒 (Hefele)，V. 1099。从已死母亲腹中取出的婴儿，若本身也已死亡，应不经洗礼，埋葬在未经祝圣的土地上。见1310年特里尔会议。↩︎

  35. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 67. 4及后，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 628及下页。↩︎

  36. Pueri non se ipsos sed per actum eccl. salutem suscipiunt，即儿童不是凭自己，而是藉教会的行动领受救恩。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 68. 9，米涅 (Migne)，646；波拿文图拉 (Bonavent.)，《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，VII.，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 版，VII. 320；邓斯·司各脱 (Duns Scotus)，见泽贝格 (Seeberg)，第360页。↩︎

  37. 彼得·伦巴德 (P. Lomb.)，IV. 6. 2，米涅 (Migne)，II. 853。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，米涅，IV. 649，以及邓斯·司各脱 (Duns Scotus，泽贝格〔Seeberg〕，第360页) 都同意：婴儿的头若已露出母腹，就可以施洗，因为头是不朽生命主体的居所。↩︎

  38. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 648。邓斯 (Duns) 提出的一个理由是：这些犹太人的子女，若受到良好教育，到了第三、第四代就会成为良好的基督徒 (vere fideles，真正的信徒)。泽贝格 (Seeberg)，第364页。↩︎

  39. 《神学大全》(Summa)，V. 6，米涅 (Migne)，176. 132。↩︎

  40. 《箴言集注》(In Sent.)，IV. 4, 3. 3，巴黎版，XVI. 406、410。↩︎

  41. 泽贝格 (Seeberg)，第359页；《神学大全》(Summa)，III. 66. 7，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 613及下页；彼得·伦巴德 (P. Lomb.)，IV. 3, 8，米涅，II. 845；波拿文图拉 (Bonav.)，《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，VII.，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 版，第319页；邓斯·司各脱 (Duns Scotus)，《箴言集第四卷注》(In IV. Sent.)，第XVI卷，第272页。德龙特海姆 (Drontheim) 大主教询问格列高利九世 (Gregory IX)：有一次因手边无水而用啤酒施行的洗礼是否有效；他作了否定的答复。波特哈斯特 (Potthast)，11,048。1278年欧里亚克 (Aurillac) 教会会议宣布，淡水、盐水或融雪水都是适宜的材料。↩︎

  42. Quamvis tutius sit baptizare per modum immersionis, potest tamen fieri baptismus per modum aspersionis vel etiam per modum effusionis. 即虽然以浸入方式施洗更为稳妥，洗礼仍可用洒水，甚至浇水的方式施行。↩︎

  43. Confirmatio est quasi ultima consummatio baptismi，即坚振仿佛是洗礼最终的完成。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 72. 11，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 693。↩︎

  44. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 73. 9，引用《以西结书》3:8：“我使你的额坚硬，敌挡他们的额。”他赞许受坚振者由另一人扶持的习俗，因为“他虽然身体已经成年，灵性却尚未成年”。↩︎

  45. Quasi omnis devotio in ecclesia est in ordine ad illud sacramentum，即教会中几乎一切虔敬活动都以这件圣礼为归向。邓斯·司各脱 (Duns Scotus)，转引自泽贝格 (Seeberg)，《教义史》(Dogmengesch.)，II. 113。↩︎

  46. 参见沙弗 (Schaff)《诗歌中的基督》(Christ in Song)，第465页及后。描绘化质说的诗节如下：——

    Verbum caro, panem verum verbo carnem efficit

    Fitque sanguis Christi merum; etsi sensus deficit

    Ad firmandum cor sincerum sola fides sufficit.

    成了肉身的道，以话语使真饼成为肉身；

    纯酒也成为基督的血；纵然感官不能领会，

    要坚固真诚的心，唯有信心便已足够。↩︎

  47. Corpus et sanguis in sacramento altaris sub speciebus panis et vini veraciter continentur, transubstantiatis pane in corpore et vino in sanguinem potestate divina. 即在祭坛圣礼中，身体与血真实地包含在饼与酒的外形之下，饼藉神的权能化质为身体，酒化质为血。↩︎

  48. 米涅 (Migne)，171. 776。↩︎

  49. 参见施瓦内 (Schwane)，第656页。↩︎

  50. 施瓦内 (Schwane)，第641页；以及罗霍尔 (Rocholl) 撰写的“鲁佩特 (Rupert)”条，见赫尔措格 (Herzog)，XV. 229及后。↩︎

  51. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 73. 4，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 701；波拿文图拉 (Bonaventura)，《神学纲要》(Brev.)，VI. 9，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 版，322：eucharistiae dedit in sacrificium oblationis, et sacramentum communionis et viaticum refectionis，即他赐下圣餐，作为奉献的祭、相交的圣礼和滋养生命的旅粮。↩︎

  52. Hostia salutaris，即救恩之祭。此处引用《以弗所书》5:2；武加大译本在该节用 hostia 一词指基督的献祭。↩︎

  53. 《论圣餐》(De euchar.)，第XIII卷，668。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aquinas)，III. 74. 1，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 705，提到卡塔弗里吉派 (Cataphrygae) 和佩普扎派 (Pepuziani) 刺破儿童身体取血，混入圣餐饼的面团；他也提到清水派 (Aquarii)，他们出于节制的考虑，只用清水。↩︎

  54. 他将变质说定义为 transitus totalis substantiae in substantiam，即一种实体完全转变为另一种实体。西伯格 (Seeberg)，第378页。↩︎

  55. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 75. 1，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 716：neque sensu, neque intellectu deprehendi potest sed sola fide，即既不能凭感官，也不能凭理智领会，惟独凭信心。波拿文图拉 (Bonaventura) 在《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，VI. 9中说：in specierum utraque continetur totaliter, non circumscriptibiliter, nec localiter sed sacramentaliter totus Christus，即在两种外形的每一种中，都包含完整的基督；这不是以受界限或处所限制的方式，而是以圣礼的方式。↩︎

  56. Quamvis non sint substantia, habent virtutem substantiae. 即它们虽不是实体，却具有实体的效能。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 77. 6，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 755。↩︎

  57. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 77. 7，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 756；波拿文图拉 (Bonaventura)，《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，322。↩︎

  58. Non solum caro sed totum corpus Christi, scilicet ossa, nervi et alia hujusmodi. 即不仅是肉，而且是基督的整个身体，包括骨、筋以及其他诸如此类的部分。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，76. 1，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 732。他强调“身体”一词，身体由各个部分构成；他将《约翰福音》6:56中的“肉”解释为代表身体。遵循亚里士多德 (Aristotle) 对实体与形式的区分，托马斯·阿奎那 (Thomas Aquinas)，米涅，IV. 726，以及其他经院学者（参见施瓦内〔Schwane〕，第648页），宣称饼和酒的形式也转变为基督的身体和血。forma 与 species 两词有所区别。饼和酒的 species（外形）依然存在，forma（形式）则消失。但斯·司各脱 (Duns Scotus) 用了大量篇幅，证明一种实体可以具有多种形式。↩︎

  59. Sub utraque specie sacramenti totus est Christus. 即在圣礼的两种外形之下，各有完整的基督。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，76. 2，米涅 (Migne)，733。Sub utraque specie est unus Christus et totus et indivisus, scilicet corpus, et anima, et Deus. 即在两种外形之下，都是同一位完整而不可分的基督，也就是身体、灵魂与神性。波拿文图拉 (Bonaventura)，《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，VI. 9，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 版，VII. 322。↩︎

  60. In acceptatione sanguinis totum Christum, Deum et hominem, et in acceptatione corporis similiter totum accipimus. 即领受血时，我们领受完整的基督，就是神而人者；领受身体时，同样领受完整的基督。《书信》(Ep.)，4:107，米涅 (Migne)，第159卷，第255页。安瑟伦 (Anselm) 在这里区分基督的身体与灵，血和酒代表灵，饼和肉代表身体。↩︎

  61. 《神学大全》(Summa)，II. VIII.，米涅 (Migne)，176. 462：ego in ejusmodi divina secreta magis venerenda quam discutienda cerneo，即我认为，这类神圣奥秘，与其加以探究，不如心存敬畏。↩︎

  62. 《神学大全》(Summa)，81. 3，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 810–813。安瑟伦 (Anselm) 也用过同样的话，米涅，159. 255。施瓦内 (Schwane) 也承认，基督吃了自己身体的这一观念，在经院学者中普遍流行，第645页。↩︎

  63. Rex sedet in coena turba, cinctus duodena

    Se tenet in manibus et cibat ipse cibus.

    君王坐席，十二人环侍；

    手中持着自己，本为食物，亲自施食。↩︎

  64. 持此说的有雨果 (Hugo)，II. 8. 4；伦巴德 (the Lombard)，XI. 8；托马斯·阿奎那 (Thomas Aquinas)，81. 2，米涅 (Migne)，第811页及下页。这种欺瞒被称为 fictio（假象），也被称为“犹大的领餐”。1175年伦敦会议。这一论证显然与福音书叙事的字面意义相矛盾。↩︎

  65. 关于这一神学与形而上学上的奇谈，参见托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，80. 3，米涅 (Migne)，789：non tangit mus ipsum corpus Christi, secundum propriam speciem sed solum secundum species sacramentales … nec tamen animal brutum sacramentaliter corpus Christi manducat quia non est natum uti eo ut sacramento, unde non sacramentaliter sed per accidens corpus Christi manducat，即老鼠并未接触基督身体本有的形态，只接触圣礼的外形……然而，无理性的动物并非以圣礼的方式吃基督的身体，因为它生来就不能将其当作圣礼使用；所以，它不是以圣礼的方式，而是偶然地吃基督的身体，等等。大阿尔伯特 (Alb. Magnus)，《〈箴言四书〉注》(In Sent.)，IV. 13. 38，博尔涅 (Borgnet) 版，XXIX. 397；波拿文图拉 (Bonaventura)，《〈箴言四书〉注》(Sent.)，IV. 13. 2. 1，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 版，V. 550。↩︎

  66. 西伯格 (Seeberg)，第360页。↩︎

  67. 祭司是中保。《神学大全》(Summa)，米涅 (Migne)，176. 472。↩︎

  68. Sacerdos gerit imaginem Christi, 即祭司代表基督的形象。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 83. 1，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 830。↩︎

  69. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，《补编》(Supplem.)，37. 5，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 1062。↩︎

  70. Ex seipso virtutem habet gratiam conferendi. 即它自身具有赐予恩典的能力。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 79. 1，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 774。↩︎

  71. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，79. 7，《补编》(Supplem.)，III. 71. 10，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 782、1246及下页；大阿尔伯特 (Al. Magnus)，I. 4，将弥撒的益处也扩及已经得荣耀的人：pro salute vivorum, pro requie defunctorum, pro gloria beatorum，即为活人的救恩、死者的安息、蒙福者的荣耀。↩︎

  73. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 83. 5，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 850。↩︎

  74. Non ex hoc desinit aliquis minister esse Christi quod est malus, 即一个人并不因其邪恶，就不再是基督的仆役。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，82. 5、7，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 821、824；安瑟伦 (Anselm)，《书信》(ep.)，IV. 107，米涅，159. 257，也说过同样的话：nec a bono sacerdote majus，即由善良祭司施行，也不会更大，等等。↩︎

  75. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 80. 12，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 809。Perfectio hujus sacramenti non est in usu fidelium sed in consecratione materiae. 即这一圣礼的完备，不在于信徒的领用，而在于对其质料的祝圣。↩︎

  76. Requiritur ut cum magna devotione et reverentia ad, hoc sacramentum accedat .... Eucharistia exigit actualem devotionem in suscipiente，即人必须以极大的虔诚和敬畏领受这一圣礼……圣餐要求领受者当下具有虔诚之心。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 80. 10，《补编》(Supplem.)，III. 32. 4，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 805、1038。↩︎

  77. 当哈纳克 (Harnack) 发出以下感叹时，我们不能不深有同感：“教会在其圣餐教义中，表达了她所珍视的一切——她的神学、她与基督的奥秘关系、信徒的相交、祭司职分、献祭；惟独没有表达那寻求确据、也获赐确据的信心。”《教义史》(Dogmengesch.)，II. 489及下页。↩︎

  78. Populus indiget medio ad Deum qui per seipsum accedere ad deum non potest. 即民众不能自行来到上帝面前，因此需要一位通向上帝的中保。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 22. 4，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 219。↩︎

  87. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，III. 80. 12，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 808及下页：nihil derogat perfectioni hujus sacr., si populus sumat corpus sine sanguine dummodo sacerdos consecrans sumat utrumque，即只要施行祝圣的祭司领受两者，民众只领身体而不领血，并不减损这一圣礼的完备。波拿文图拉 (Bonaventura) 也持此说，《〈箴言四书〉注》(Sent.)，IV. 11. 2。↩︎

  103. 伦巴德 (the Lombard) 论告解礼的篇幅，是论圣餐的两倍半：米涅 (Migne) 版，第868–899页，而论圣餐为第856–868页；圣维克多的雨果 (Hugo of St. Victor)，米涅版，第550–578页，而论圣餐为第462–471页；托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aquinas)，米涅版，第852–1023页，而论圣餐为第695–852页；波拿文图拉 (Bonaventura) 论告解礼的篇幅则几乎为论圣餐的四倍：佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 版，第V卷，533–709，第VI卷，1–129，而论圣餐为第V卷，415–533。↩︎

  104. Absolvitur homo a peccato, et reunitur ecclesiae et reconciliatur Christo, mediante clavi sacerdotali, 即借着祭司的钥匙权，人得以脱离罪，与教会重新联合，并与基督和好。波拿文图拉 (Bonaventura)，《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，VI. 10，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 版，VII. 323。↩︎

  105. 兰斯译本 (Rheims Version) 将这个词译为“行补赎”，虽然并非一贯如此，却由此使英语读者完全陷入混淆，不自觉地将教会所创设的圣礼观念，读进新约的这个词中。↩︎

  106. Poenitentia dicitur et sacramentum et virtus mentis，即 poenitentia 既指一种圣礼，也指心灵的一种德性。伦巴德 (Lombard)，XIV. 1，第869页；托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 850及后。我们使用“悔改”与“补赎”两个词，经院学者却只用 poenitentia 一个词，因而使人的理解陷入迷惑，仿佛内心的悔改，即 μετάνοια*，并未涵盖原词的全部意义。↩︎

  107. 《箴言四书》(Sent.)，XVII. 1，米涅 (Migne)，第880页。告解礼理论的完备，在很大程度上得力于《论真假悔改》(de vera et falsa poenitentia) 这篇短论；它可能成书于十二世纪，却被冒托为奥古斯丁 (Augustine) 的著作。格拉提安 (Gratian) 几乎将其全文收入自己的《教令集》(Decretals)，彼得·伦巴德 (Peter the Lombard) 也同样如此。据利 (Lea)，I. 210，直到十七世纪，人们仍将此书作为奥古斯丁的著作引用。利认为，此书出自分别生活于五世纪和十二世纪的两位作者之手。↩︎

  108. 米勒 (Müller) 的名著《转变》(Der Umschwung) 等，说明了这一点。阿伯拉尔 (Abaelard) 代表旧观点的陈述，以及托马斯·阿奎那 (Thomas Aquinas) 代表新观点的陈述，均见克勒 (Köhler)，第11–18页。↩︎

  109. 《神学大全》(Summa)，III. 84. 7；《补编》(Supplem.)，VIII. 1，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 864、943。↩︎

  110. 伦巴德 (Lombard)，XVI. 1，米涅 (Migne)，第877页；大阿尔伯特 (Alb. Magnus)，博尔涅 (Borgnet) 版，XXIX. 536。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，90. 1、2，米涅，IV. 912及下页，以及波拿文图拉 (Bonaventura)，《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，VI. 10，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 版，VII. 323，也将前三项称为告解礼的“完整构成部分”。阿伯拉尔 (Abaelard) 亦然，《伦理学》(Ethica)，17–24。↩︎

  111. 参见施瓦内 (Schwane) 对这一观点的严厉谴责；他断言，这无疑就是伦巴德 (the Lombard) 的观点，第662页。↩︎

  112. Timor servilis principium est attritionis, 即奴仆式的恐惧是不完全痛悔的起点。黑尔斯的亚历山大 (Alex. of Hales)，引自施瓦内 (Schwane)，第664页。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aquinas)，《补编》(Supplem.)，I. 2，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 919，比亚历山大、波拿文图拉 (Bonaventura) 和但斯 (Duns) 温和得多。海斯特巴赫的凯撒 (Caesar of Heisterbach) 将“奴仆式的恐惧”称为“上帝的恩赐”，克尼格 (Koeniger)，第31页。中世纪末期，加布里埃尔·比尔 (Gabriel Biel) 主张，不完全痛悔借着认罪和赦罪转变为完全痛悔。参见西伯格 (Seeberg)，《教义史》(Dogmengesch.)，II. 121。↩︎

  113. 参见哈恩 (Hahn)，第413页；施瓦内 (Schwane)，第666页。特伦托会议 (council of Trent)，XIV. 4（沙弗的《信经集》〔Schaff’s Creeds〕，II. 145及下页），采用“不完全痛悔”一词，并将其定义为不完备的痛悔。扬森 (Janssen) 的著作引发了一场关于赎罪券的激烈争论，科尔德 (Kolde)、卡韦劳 (Kawerau)、迪克霍夫 (Dieckhoff) 等人参与其中，attritio（不完全痛悔）的教义成为讨论的中心之一。哈纳克 (Harnmack) 对这一教义极为严厉，视其为天主教体系内部的腐朽，《教义史》(Dogmengesch.)，II. 482、504及后。↩︎

  114. 阿奎那 (Aquinas) 引用了奥古斯丁 (Augustine) 的定义，《补编》(Supplem.)，VII. 1、IX. 3，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 940、954。↩︎

Sanctus et custodiet vos a peccatis et ab omnibus malis et post hanc vitam perducat vos in consortium omnium sanctorum，即愿父、子、圣灵赐你们一切……的宽免与赦免，保守你们脱离罪恶与一切灾祸，并在今生之后引领你们进入众圣徒的团契。利 (Lea)，I. 206。↩︎

  128. Si non potest remittere quantum ad poenam temporalem, nullo modo remittere potest quod omnino contrarium dictus evangelii，即若不能就暂罚予以赦免，便根本不能赦免，而这完全违背福音的教导。《补编》(Supplem.)，VIII. 2，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 988；《箴言集注》(Sent.)，IV. 20, 1, 1-5。↩︎

  141. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，《神学大全》(Summa)，III. 83, 1。quorum meritorum tanta est copia quod omnem poenam debitam nunc viventibus excedunt，即他们的功德如此丰盛，超过现今活着的人所应受的一切刑罚。参见戈特洛布 (Gottlob)，第271页及后。↩︎

  142. Quanto plures ex ejus applicatione trahuntur ad justitiam, tanto magis accresit ipsorum cumulus meritorum，即藉此运用而被引向义的人越多，他们所积累的功德就越发增多。参见弗里德贝格 (Friedberg)，《教会法大全》(Corp. Jur. can.)，II. 1304及下页。↩︎

  146. 参见萨巴蒂埃 (Sabatier)，《巴尔托利修士》(Fr. F. Bartholi) 等，部分重印于克勒 (Köhler)，第27页及后。↩︎

  147. 若干实例见布里格尔 (Brieger)，尤其见利 (Lea)，III. 55–80。利引用《农夫皮尔斯的信经》(Piers the Ploughman’s Crede)，表明这表达了民间的信念。

    他们口中搬弄使徒的权柄，

    为的是卖掉罪孽，换取银钱或别的报酬；

    紫袍者堂皇地赦免刑罚——a pena，

    也赦免罪责——a culpa，好让他们攫取

    钱财或值钱之物，收取酬金。

    此类赎罪券最显著的实例之一，是亚历山大五世 (Alexander V.) 颁给比萨会议 (council of Pisa) 与会者的 absolutio plenaria a poena et culpa，即罪罚全赦。见冯·德尔·哈特 (Von der Hardt)，《康斯坦茨会议》(Conc. Const.)，III. 688。↩︎

  156. Per modum complementi superponitur episcopatus，即主教职作为补充而置于其上，等等；波拿文都拉 (Bonavent.)，《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，VI. 12。彼得·伦巴德 (P. Lombardus)，《箴言四书》(Sent.)，XXIV. 9，米涅 (Migne)，第904页，论及主教的四个等级，即牧首、大主教、都主教和主教。他说，这些不是圣秩，而是“尊位与职务的名称”。但斯·司各脱 (Duns Scotus) 的教导并不确定。他在一处断言，主教职必然是一个独立的圣秩，即第八级，因为有几项圣礼唯有主教才能施行。参见西伯格 (Seeberg)，第441页。另一方面，他引用耶柔米，表明主教职是教会设立的，并非神法所规定。参见施瓦内 (Schwane)，第684页。主教职是否为独立的圣秩，教会法至今仍未确定。参见弗里德贝格 (Friedberg)，《教会法》(Kirchenrecht)，第150页。特伦托会议 (council of Trent) 虽然谈到主教、司铎和执事的圣统制，却未正式裁定这一问题。参见沙弗 (Schaff)，《信经》(Creeds)，II. 186及后。英诺森三世 (Innocent III.) 将副执事列入高级圣秩。弗里德贝格，第150页。据菲利普·赫尔根勒特 (Philip Hergenröther)，《天主教教会法》(Kathol. Kirchenrecht)，第208页及下页，现今罗马天主教会普遍将主教职视为一个独立的圣职品级。↩︎

  157. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，《补编》(Supplem.)，XL. 1，米涅 (Migne)，1071；波拿文都拉 (Bonaventura)，《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，VI. 12，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 编本，327。1102年伦敦 (London) 会议、1078年苏瓦松 (Soissons) 会议、1190年鲁昂 (Rouen) 会议、1215年第四次拉特兰会议 (Fourth Lateran) 等，均规定不得遮掩剃发处。↩︎

  159. 施瓦内 (Schwane)，第681页，说中世纪的按立礼仪并无发展。托马斯·阿奎那 (Thomas Aquinas) 仅在论补赎的章节中附带提及按手。《神学大全》(Summa)，III. 84, 3，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 850。1438年佛罗伦萨会议 (council of Florence) 规定，在授予某些圣秩的祝圣礼上，应授予圣爵和圣盘。↩︎

  160. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，《补编》(Supplem.)，XXXVI. I，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 1051，si malus ordinatur nihilominus ordinationem habet，即恶人若受按立，仍然具有所受的圣秩。↩︎

  161. 例如，第9次（赫费勒〔Hefele〕，V. 380）和第11次大公会议，均作出此类判决，并指名有关的敌对教皇。1095年皮亚琴察会议 (synod of Piacenza) 也是如此，宣布维贝尔特 (Wibert) 及其他主教所施行的按立无效。↩︎

  162. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，《补编》(Supplem.)，XXXIX. 2，米涅 (Migne)，1065。Episcopus in haeresin lapsus ... non amisit potestatem quam habebat ordines conferendi，即陷入异端的主教……并未失去他原有的授予圣秩之权。托马斯在这个问题上的措辞极为坚决，并继续说：Omnis protestas quae datur cum aliqua consecratione, nulla casu contingente tolli protest, etc .... Unde cum episcopalisprotestas cum quadam consecratione detur, oportet quod perpetuo maneat quantumcumque aliquis peccet, vel ab ecclesia praecidatur，即凡藉某种祝圣而授予的权柄，都不能因任何意外情形而被取消，等等……因此，既然主教的权柄是藉某种祝圣而授予的，无论一个人怎样犯罪，或被教会剪除，这权柄都必然永久存留。↩︎

  163. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，《神学大全》(Summa)，III. 65, 2，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 598，quia minimum habet de spiritualitate，即因为它所具有的属灵性质最少。↩︎

  164. 阿伯拉尔 (Abaelard)，《基督教神学》(Theol Christ.)，31，conjugium non confert aliquod donum sicut cetera sacramenta faciunt sed tamen mali remedium est ... datur propter incontinentiam refraenendam，即婚姻不像其他圣礼那样赋予某种恩赐，却是对恶的补救……其设立是为约束放纵。圣维克多的雨果 (Hugo of St. Vict.)，《论圣礼》(De sacr.)，II. 11. 3，米涅 (Migne)，第481页，conjugium ante peccatum ad officium, post peccatum ad remedium，即犯罪以前，婚姻是为履行职分；犯罪以后，则是为补救。里尔的阿兰 (Alanus ab Insulis)，《神学规则》(Reg. Theol.)，114，米涅编本，第681页，conjugium sacramentum remedii contra incontinentiam，即婚姻是补救放纵的圣礼。波拿文都拉 (Bonaventura)，《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，VI. 13；托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，《补编》(Supplem.)，XLII. 2，米涅，IV. 1084；《神学大全》(Summa)，LXI. 2，米涅，第558页，也持同样看法。↩︎

  168. 这是指一方为天主教徒，另一方为异端者、犹太人或不信者的婚姻。↩︎

  169. 1102年、1125年、1200年伦敦 (London) 会议，1215年第四次拉特兰会议 (Fourth Lateran)，1227年特里尔 (Treves) 会议，1261年马格德堡 (Magdeburg) 会议，等等。1200年伦敦会议禁止丈夫或妻子未经对方同意而远行。托马斯·阿奎那 (Thomas Aquinas) 认为，不应准许信徒与不信者结婚，因为这种婚姻妨碍对子女施以敬拜上帝的教育和训练，而这正是该圣礼的主要目的之一。《补编》(Supplem.)，LIX. 1，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 1167。↩︎

  170. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，《补编》(Supplem.)，LXI. 2，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 1177。托马斯断言，在 carnalis copula（肉体交合）发生以前，婚姻的纽带是属灵的，任何一方都可以藉着在属灵意义上死去，即向世界死、在修道院中向上帝活，而解除这一纽带。交合之后，夫妻间的纽带便是肉体的联结——vinculum carnale——唯有肉身的死亡才能将其解除。↩︎

  171. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，《补编》(Supplem.)，LXII. 5，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 1184，non licet uni, altero vivente, ad aliam copulam transire，即只要对方尚在人世，任何一方都不得另结婚姻。然而，任何一方均可不经对方同意而进入修道院。↩︎

  172. Carnalibus appetitis infecta，即受肉欲沾染。《论受孕》(de conceptu)，II。米涅 (Migne)，158. 434。↩︎

  173. vitium concupiscentiae, quod est originale peccatum，即情欲的败坏，也就是原罪。↩︎

  174. Post peccatum non valet fieri carnalis copula absque libidinosa concupiscentia quae semper vitium est et etiam culpa，即犯罪以后，肉体交合便不可能不伴随淫欲的贪恋，而这种贪恋始终是败坏，也是罪责。彼得·伦巴德 (P. Lomb.)，《箴言四书》(Sent.)，II. 31, 3。↩︎

  175. Etsi anima non traducatur, quia virtus seminis non potest causare animam rationalem，即虽然灵魂并非由生殖传递，因为精液的能力不能产生理性的灵魂。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aquinas)，《神学大全》(Summa)，II. 81, 1，米涅 (Migne)，II. 629。↩︎

  176. Carentia ... defectus originalis justitiae estoriginal peccatum，即缺乏……原初之义的缺失就是原罪。施瓦内 (Schwane)，第401页；托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，《神学大全》(Summa)，II. 81, 5。↩︎

  177. Spoliatio in gratuitis et vulneratio in naturalibus，即恩赐被剥夺，天性受损伤。↩︎

  178. 《神学大全》(Summa)，II. 82, 3，materialiter quidem est concupiscentia, formaliter vero est defectus orig. just.，即就质料而言，它是情欲；就形式而言，它则是原初之义的缺失。Vitium（败坏）和 corruptio（腐化）是最常用于描述罪之道德性质的词。圣维克多的雨果 (Hugo of St. Victor)，《论圣礼》(De sacr.)，I. 28，米涅 (Migne)，176. 299。↩︎

  179. Peccatum orig. non contrahitur a matre sed a patre，即原罪不是从母亲、而是从父亲承受的，等等。《神学大全》(Summa)，II. 81, 5。↩︎

  180. 彼得·伦巴德 (P. Lomb.)，II. 42, 9；大阿尔伯特 (Alb. Magnus)，博尔涅 (Borgnet) 编本，XXVII. 663及后，等等。↩︎

  181. 彼得·伦巴德 (P. Lomb.)，II. 42，将它们列为 inanis gloria, ira, invidia, acedia vel tristitia, avaritia, gastrimargia, luxuria，即虚荣、愤怒、嫉妒、怠惰或忧郁、贪婪、暴食、淫欲。大阿尔伯特 (Albertus Magnus) 巧妙地讨论了其数目是否可能超过七种。《箴言集注》(In Sent.)，II. 42，博尔涅 (Borgnet) 编本，XXVII. 662及后。↩︎

  182. Utrum aequalis fieret multiplicatio virorum et mulierum，即男人与女人是否会以相同数目繁衍。《箴言集注》(In Sent.)，II. 20, 2，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 编本，III. 85。波拿文都拉 (Bonaventura) 为解释性别差异所提出的三个理由，须由读者自行阅读原文。他探讨这个题目时，陈述精确，细节详尽，正体现了经院讨论的特色。平心而论，他确曾宣称这是一个难题，当时的医师与自然哲学家对此意见甚为分歧。↩︎

  183. Sed dolo illo serpentino quo mulier seducta est, nullo modo arbitror illum potuisse seduci，即但我认为，那诱惑了女人的蛇之诡计，绝不可能同样诱惑他。↩︎

  187. Man hatte Augustinische Formeln und gregorianische Gedanken，即人们采用的是奥古斯丁式的公式，所怀的却是格列高利式的思想。卢夫斯 (Loofs)，第291页。施瓦内 (Schwane)，第455页，赞扬托马斯对恩典教义的清晰论述，并说他的教导与今日天主教教义学体系中的教导相同。在我看来，卢夫斯、哈纳克 (Harnack) 和西伯格 (Seeberg) 将一种去奥古斯丁化的倾向归于托马斯，似乎言之过甚。他关于神恩之必要与人之无能的明确陈述，已充分体现奥古斯丁的思想。在研究过托马斯的圣礼理论之后，很容易对他关于恩典的陈述作出伯拉纠主义的解释。更公允的做法，是根据他对恩典教义的教导来解释他的圣礼理论。↩︎

  188. meritum apud deum esse non potest, nisisecundum praesuppositionem divinae ordinationis，即若不以上帝的安排为前提，在上帝面前就不可能有功德。《神学大全》(Summa)，II. 114, I，米涅 (Migne)，II. 960。↩︎

  189. Verum non potest cognosecre sine auxilio divino，即没有上帝的帮助，人就不能认识真理。《神学大全》(Summa)，II. 109, 2, 6, 7，米涅 (Migne)，II. 907及后。↩︎

  190. Sanguis qui est pretium nostrae redemptionis non dicitur obtulisse diabolo sed deo，即作为我们救赎代价的血，并不是说献给了魔鬼，而是献给了上帝。《神学大全》(Summa)，III. 48, 4，米涅 (Migne)，III. 44。↩︎

  191. Mors Christi nos justificat, dum per eam charitas excitatur in cordibus nostris，即基督的死使我们称义，因为借着他的死，爱在我们心中被激发。《箴言集》(Sent.)，III. 19, 1。↩︎

  192. Per passionem Christi homo cognoscit quantum deus hominem diligat et per hoc provocatur ad eumdiligendum，即借着基督的受难，人认识到上帝何等爱人，并由此受到激励去爱他。《神学大全》(Summa)，III. 46, 3，米涅 (Migne)，III. 417。↩︎

  210. Gehenna illa quae stagnum ignis et sulphuris dicta est, corporeus ignis erit et cruciabit damnatorum corpora vel hominum vel daemonum，即那被称为硫磺火湖的地狱，将是有形体的火，要折磨被定罪者的身体，无论是人还是魔鬼。彼得·伦巴德 (P. Lomb.)，《箴言集》(Sent.)，IV. 44, 6。Absque dubietate corporeus ignis cruciat，即毫无疑问，有形体的火施行折磨，等等。大阿尔伯特 (Alb. Mag.)，《箴言集注》(In Sent.)，博尔涅 (Borgnet) 编本，XXX. 393。Ignis corporalis qui concremabit et affliget spiritus et etiam corpora ... sed semper affliget, alios plus alios minus, secundum exigentiam meritorum，即有形体的火将焚烧并折磨灵，也折磨身体……而且要永远折磨，按各人行为所应得的，有的较重，有的较轻。波拿文都拉 (Bonavent.)，《神学纲要》(Brev.)，VII. 6。↩︎

  211. Probabile est, quod idem locus vel quasi continuus, sit infernus et limbus，即地狱与灵薄狱很可能是同一处地方，或几乎彼此相连。托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 1222。托马斯交替使用 infernus 和 inferni 来称呼地狱诸境。大阿尔伯特 (Alb. Magnus) 则使用中性复数 inferna。《箴言集注》(In Sent.)，III, 26, C. 392。↩︎

  212. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，《神学大全》(Summa)，III. 69, 6，originali peccato debebatur poena aeterna in limbo puerorum，即原罪所应受的是在婴孩灵薄狱中的永刑。Limbus 意为边缘或边界。大阿尔伯特 (Alb. Magnus) 也称其为 limbus parvulorum，即幼儿之境。博尔涅 (Borgnet) 编本，XXVIII. 392。↩︎

Aq.)，《补编》(Supplem.)，第1223页。divinae justitiae aequitas perpetualiter eos consolidat, ut nec proficiant, nec deficiant, nec laetentur, nec tristentur; sed semper per sic uniformiter maneant，即上帝公义的公平永远使他们处于固定状态，既不长进，也不衰退，既不喜乐，也不忧伤，而始终如此，维持同一状态，等等。波拿文都拉 (Bonavent.)，《箴言集注》(In Sent.)，II. 33, 2, 3，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 编本，III. 419。↩︎

  220. 托马斯·阿奎那 (Th. Aq.)，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 1239。↩︎

  221. Claritas, subtilitas, agilitas, et impossibilitas quae ... secundum majoritatem et minoritatem prius habitae charitatis，即光明、精微、敏捷与不可能性……按先前所具有的爱之大小而定。《神学纲要》(Brevil.)，VII. 7，佩尔蒂埃 (Peltier) 编本，VII. 340。↩︎

  222. Deum per essentiam videre in quo consistit perfecta hominis beatitudo，即直观上帝的本质，人的完全福乐就在于此。《神学大全》(Summa)，III. 52, 5，米涅 (Migne)，IV. 482。↩︎

  223. 《讲道集》(Serm.)，XI。↩︎

  224. 《宣讲篇》(Proslog.)，XXIV及后。↩︎

  225. 1206年（An.），卢阿德 (Luard) 编马修·巴黎 (M. Paris) 著作，II. 497–512。↩︎

  226. 《对话录》(Dial)，I. 32，斯特兰奇 (Strange) 编本，I. 36–39。↩︎

  227. 见瓦尔特 (Walter)，《最早的巡回传道人》(Die ersten Wanderprediger) 等，第49页。↩︎

  228. 见考夫曼 (Kaufmann)，《尚坦普雷的托马斯》(Thos. von Chantimpré)，第117及下页。↩︎
